+struct MpiContext {
+    int rank = 0;
+    int size = 1;
+
+    bool isRoot() const { return rank == 0; }
+};
+
+struct RowPartition {
+    size_t first = 0;
+    size_t count = 0;
+    std::vector<int> counts;
+    std::vector<int> displacements;
+
+    int ownerOf(size_t globalRow) const {
+        for (size_t rank = 0; rank < counts.size(); ++rank) {
+            const size_t firstRow = static_cast<size_t>(displacements[rank]);
+            const size_t rowCount = static_cast<size_t>(counts[rank]);
+            if (globalRow >= firstRow && globalRow < firstRow + rowCount) {
+                return static_cast<int>(rank);
+            }
+        }
+        return 0;
+    }
 };
 
+RowPartition makeRowPartition(size_t numRows, const MpiContext& mpi) {
+    RowPartition partition;
+    partition.counts.resize(static_cast<size_t>(mpi.size));
+    partition.displacements.resize(static_cast<size_t>(mpi.size));
+
+    const size_t processCount = static_cast<size_t>(mpi.size);
+    const size_t baseCount = numRows / processCount;
+    const size_t remainder = numRows % processCount;
+    size_t displacement = 0;
+    for (int rank = 0; rank < mpi.size; ++rank) {
+        const size_t rankCount = baseCount + (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+        partition.counts[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(rankCount);
+        partition.displacements[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(displacement);
+        if (rank == mpi.rank) {
+            partition.first = displacement;
+            partition.count = rankCount;
+        }
+        displacement += rankCount;
+    }
+    return partition;
+}

 // Random Number Generation
 // ============================================================================
 
+// A counter-based generator makes samples independent of the rank that owns a
+// form-factor entry.  The counter is the position in the original row-major
+// sampling stream, so MPI process counts cannot change the random sequence.
 class RandomGenerator {
-    std::mt19937 rng;
-    std::uniform_real_distribution<val_t> dist;
+    uint64_t counter;
+
+    static uint64_t mix(uint64_t value) {
+        value += 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;
+        value = (value ^ (value >> 30)) * 0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9ULL;
+        value = (value ^ (value >> 27)) * 0x94d049bb133111ebULL;
+        return value ^ (value >> 31);
+    }
+
 public:
-    explicit RandomGenerator(uint32_t seed = 42) : rng(seed), dist(0.0f, 1.0f) {}
-    val_t rand() { return dist(rng); }
+    explicit RandomGenerator(uint64_t startCounter) : counter(startCounter) {}
+
+    val_t rand() {
+        // Use the top 24 bits, exactly representable in a float, for a
+        // uniformly distributed value in [0, 1).
+        constexpr val_t INV_2_POW_24 = 1.0f / 16777216.0f;
+        return static_cast<val_t>(mix(counter++) >> 40) * INV_2_POW_24;
+    }
 };

+struct MpiContext {
+    int rank = 0;
+    int size = 1;
+
+    bool isRoot() const { return rank == 0; }
+};
+
+struct RowPartition {
+    size_t first = 0;
+    size_t count = 0;
+    std::vector<int> counts;
+    std::vector<int> displacements;
+
+    int ownerOf(size_t globalRow) const {
+        for (size_t rank = 0; rank < counts.size(); ++rank) {
+            const size_t firstRow = static_cast<size_t>(displacements[rank]);
+            const size_t rowCount = static_cast<size_t>(counts[rank]);
+            if (globalRow >= firstRow && globalRow < firstRow + rowCount) {
+                return static_cast<int>(rank);
+            }
+        }
+        return 0;
+    }
 };
 
+RowPartition makeRowPartition(size_t numRows, const MpiContext& mpi) {
+    RowPartition partition;
+    partition.counts.resize(static_cast<size_t>(mpi.size));
+    partition.displacements.resize(static_cast<size_t>(mpi.size));
+
+    const size_t processCount = static_cast<size_t>(mpi.size);
+    const size_t baseCount = numRows / processCount;
+    const size_t remainder = numRows % processCount;
+    size_t displacement = 0;
+    for (int rank = 0; rank < mpi.size; ++rank) {
+        const size_t rankCount = baseCount + (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+        partition.counts[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(rankCount);
+        partition.displacements[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(displacement);
+        if (rank == mpi.rank) {
+            partition.first = displacement;
+            partition.count = rankCount;
+        }
+        displacement += rankCount;
+    }
+    return partition;
+}

 // Random Number Generation
 // ============================================================================
 
+// A counter-based generator makes samples independent of the rank that owns a
+// form-factor entry.  The counter is the position in the original row-major
+// sampling stream, so MPI process counts cannot change the random sequence.
 class RandomGenerator {
-    std::mt19937 rng;
-    std::uniform_real_distribution<val_t> dist;
+    uint64_t counter;
+
+    static uint64_t mix(uint64_t value) {
+        value += 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;
+        value = (value ^ (value >> 30)) * 0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9ULL;
+        value = (value ^ (value >> 27)) * 0x94d049bb133111ebULL;
+        return value ^ (value >> 31);
+    }
+
 public:
-    explicit RandomGenerator(uint32_t seed = 42) : rng(seed), dist(0.0f, 1.0f) {}
-    val_t rand() { return dist(rng); }
+    explicit RandomGenerator(uint64_t startCounter) : counter(startCounter) {}
+
+    val_t rand() {
+        // Use the top 24 bits, exactly representable in a float, for a
+        // uniformly distributed value in [0, 1).
+        constexpr val_t INV_2_POW_24 = 1.0f / 16777216.0f;
+        return static_cast<val_t>(mix(counter++) >> 40) * INV_2_POW_24;
+    }
 };

+struct MpiContext {
+    int rank = 0;
+    int size = 1;
+
+    bool isRoot() const { return rank == 0; }
+};
+
+struct RowPartition {
+    size_t first = 0;
+    size_t count = 0;
+    std::vector<int> counts;
+    std::vector<int> displacements;
+
+    int ownerOf(size_t globalRow) const {
+        for (size_t rank = 0; rank < counts.size(); ++rank) {
+            const size_t firstRow = static_cast<size_t>(displacements[rank]);
+            const size_t rowCount = static_cast<size_t>(counts[rank]);
+            if (globalRow >= firstRow && globalRow < firstRow + rowCount) {
+                return static_cast<int>(rank);
+            }
+        }
+        return 0;
+    }
 };
 
+RowPartition makeRowPartition(size_t numRows, const MpiContext& mpi) {
+    RowPartition partition;
+    partition.counts.resize(static_cast<size_t>(mpi.size));
+    partition.displacements.resize(static_cast<size_t>(mpi.size));
+
+    const size_t processCount = static_cast<size_t>(mpi.size);
+    const size_t baseCount = numRows / processCount;
+    const size_t remainder = numRows % processCount;
+    size_t displacement = 0;
+    for (int rank = 0; rank < mpi.size; ++rank) {
+        const size_t rankCount = baseCount + (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+        partition.counts[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(rankCount);

 // Random Number Generation
 // ============================================================================
 
+// A counter-based generator makes samples independent of the rank that owns a
+// form-factor entry.  The counter is the position in the original row-major
+// sampling stream, so MPI process counts cannot change the random sequence.
 class RandomGenerator {
-    std::mt19937 rng;
-    std::uniform_real_distribution<val_t> dist;
+    uint64_t counter;
+
+    static uint64_t mix(uint64_t value) {
+        value += 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;
+        value = (value ^ (value >> 30)) * 0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9ULL;
+        value = (value ^ (value >> 27)) * 0x94d049bb133111ebULL;
+        return value ^ (value >> 31);
+    }

-    explicit RandomGenerator(uint32_t seed = 42) : rng(seed), dist(0.0f, 1.0f) {}
-    val_t rand() { return dist(rng); }
+    explicit RandomGenerator(uint64_t startCounter) : counter(startCounter) {}
+
+    val_t rand() {
+        // Use the top 24 bits, exactly representable in a float, for a
+        // uniformly distributed value in [0, 1).
+        constexpr val_t INV_2_POW_24 = 1.0f / 16777216.0f;
+        return static_cast<val_t>(mix(counter++) >> 40) * INV_2_POW_24;
+    }

+struct MpiContext {
+    int rank = 0;
+    int size = 1;

+    bool isRoot() const { return rank == 0; }

+struct RowPartition {
+    size_t first = 0;
+    size_t count = 0;
+    std::vector<int> counts;
+    std::vector<int> displacements;
+
+    int ownerOf(size_t globalRow) const {
+        for (size_t rank = 0; rank < counts.size(); ++rank) {
+            const size_t firstRow = static_cast<size_t>(displacements[rank]);
+            const size_t rowCount = static_cast<size_t>(counts[rank]);
+            if (globalRow >= firstRow && globalRow < firstRow + rowCount) {
+                return static_cast<int>(rank);
+            }
+        }
+        return 0;
+    }
 };
 
+RowPartition makeRowPartition(size_t numRows, const MpiContext& mpi) {
+    RowPartition partition;
+    partition.counts.resize(static_cast<size_t>(mpi.size));
+    partition.displacements.resize(static_cast<size_t>(mpi.size));
+
+    const size_t processCount = static_cast<size_t>(mpi.size);
+    const size_t baseCount = numRows / processCount;
+    const size_t remainder = numRows % processCount;
+    size_t displacement = 0;
+    for (int rank = 0; rank < mpi.size; ++rank) {
+        const size_t rankCount = baseCount + (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+        partition.counts[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(rankCount);
+        partition.displacements[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(displacement);
+        if (rank == mpi.rank) {
+            partition.first = displacement;
+            partition.count = rankCount;
+        }
+        displacement += rankCount;
+    }
+    return partition;
+}

 // Random Number Generation
 // ============================================================================
 
+// A counter-based generator makes samples independent of the rank that owns a
+// form-factor entry.  The counter is the position in the original row-major
+// sampling stream, so MPI process counts cannot change the random sequence.
 class RandomGenerator {
-    std::mt19937 rng;
-    std::uniform_real_distribution<val_t> dist;
+    uint64_t counter;
+
+    static uint64_t mix(uint64_t value) {
+        value += 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;
+        value = (value ^ (value >> 30)) * 0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9ULL;
+        value = (value ^ (value >> 27)) * 0x94d049bb133111ebULL;
+        return value ^ (value >> 31);
+    }
+
 public:
-    explicit RandomGenerator(uint32_t seed = 42) : rng(seed), dist(0.0f, 1.0f) {}
-    val_t rand() { return dist(rng); }
+    explicit RandomGenerator(uint64_t startCounter) : counter(startCounter) {}
+
+    val_t rand() {
+        // Use the top 24 bits, exactly representable in a float, for a
+        // uniformly distributed value in [0, 1).
+        constexpr val_t INV_2_POW_24 = 1.0f / 16777216.0f;
+        return static_cast<val_t>(mix(counter++) >> 40) * INV_2_POW_24;
+    }
 };

+struct MpiContext {
+    int rank = 0;
+    int size = 1;
+
+    bool isRoot() const { return rank == 0; }
+};
+
+struct RowPartition {
+    size_t first = 0;
+    size_t count = 0;
+    std::vector<int> counts;
+    std::vector<int> displacements;
+
+    int ownerOf(size_t globalRow) const {
+        for (size_t rank = 0; rank < counts.size(); ++rank) {
+            const size_t firstRow = static_cast<size_t>(displacements[rank]);
+            const size_t rowCount = static_cast<size_t>(counts[rank]);
+            if (globalRow >= firstRow && globalRow < firstRow + rowCount) {
+                return static_cast<int>(rank);
+            }
+        }
+        return 0;
+    }
 };
 
+RowPartition makeRowPartition(size_t numRows, const MpiContext& mpi) {
+    RowPartition partition;
+    partition.counts.resize(static_cast<size_t>(mpi.size));
+    partition.displacements.resize(static_cast<size_t>(mpi.size));
+
+    const size_t processCount = static_cast<size_t>(mpi.size);
+    const size_t baseCount = numRows / processCount;
+    const size_t remainder = numRows % processCount;
+    size_t displacement = 0;
+    for (int rank = 0; rank < mpi.size; ++rank) {
+        const size_t rankCount = baseCount + (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+        partition.counts[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(rankCount);
+        partition.displacements[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(displacement);
+        if (rank == mpi.rank) {
+            partition.first = displacement;
+            partition.count = rankCount;
+        }
+        displacement += rankCount;
+    }
+    return partition;
+}
+

 // Random Number Generation
 // ============================================================================
 
+// A counter-based generator makes samples independent of the rank that owns a
+// form-factor entry.  The counter is the position in the original row-major
+// sampling stream, so MPI process counts cannot change the random sequence.
 class RandomGenerator {
-    std::mt19937 rng;
-    std::uniform_real_distribution<val_t> dist;
+    uint64_t counter;
+
+    static uint64_t mix(uint64_t value) {
+        value += 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;
+        value = (value ^ (value >> 30)) * 0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9ULL;
+        value = (value ^ (value >> 27)) * 0x94d049bb133111ebULL;
+        return value ^ (value >> 31);
+    }
+
 public:
-    explicit RandomGenerator(uint32_t seed = 42) : rng(seed), dist(0.0f, 1.0f) {}
-    val_t rand() { return dist(rng); }
+    explicit RandomGenerator(uint64_t startCounter) : counter(startCounter) {}
+
+    val_t rand() {
+        // Use the top 24 bits, exactly representable in a float, for a
+        // uniformly distributed value in [0, 1).
+        constexpr val_t INV_2_POW_24 = 1.0f / 16777216.0f;
+        return static_cast<val_t>(mix(counter++) >> 40) * INV_2_POW_24;
+    }
 };

+struct MpiContext {
+    int rank = 0;
+    int size = 1;
+
+    bool isRoot() const { return rank == 0; }
+};
+
+struct RowPartition {
+    size_t first = 0;
+    size_t count = 0;
+    std::vector<int> counts;
+    std::vector<int> displacements;
+
+    int ownerOf(size_t globalRow) const {
+        for (size_t rank = 0; rank < counts.size(); ++rank) {
+            const size_t firstRow = static_cast<size_t>(displacements[rank]);
+            const size_t rowCount = static_cast<size_t>(counts[rank]);
+            if (globalRow >= firstRow && globalRow < firstRow + rowCount) {
+                return static_cast<int>(rank);
+            }
+        }
+        return 0;
+    }
 };
 
+RowPartition makeRowPartition(size_t numRows, const MpiContext& mpi) {
+    RowPartition partition;
+    partition.counts.resize(static_cast<size_t>(mpi.size));
+    partition.displacements.resize(static_cast<size_t>(mpi.size));
+
+    const size_t processCount = static_cast<size_t>(mpi.size);
+    const size_t baseCount = numRows / processCount;
+    const size_t remainder = numRows % processCount;
+    size_t displacement = 0;
+    for (int rank = 0; rank < mpi.size; ++rank) {
+        const size_t rankCount = baseCount + (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+        partition.counts[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(rankCount);
+        partition.displacements[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(displacement);
+        if (rank == mpi.rank) {
+            partition.first = displacement;
+            partition.count = rankCount;
+        }
+        displacement += rankCount;
+    }
+    return partition;
+}
+

 // Random Number Generation
 // ============================================================================
 
+// A counter-based generator makes samples independent of the rank that owns a
+// form-factor entry.  The counter is the position in the original row-major
+// sampling stream, so MPI process counts cannot change the random sequence.
 class RandomGenerator {
-    std::mt19937 rng;
-    std::uniform_real_distribution<val_t> dist;
+    uint64_t counter;
+
+    static uint64_t mix(uint64_t value) {
+        value += 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;
+        value = (value ^ (value >> 30)) * 0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9ULL;
+        value = (value ^ (value >> 27)) * 0x94d049bb133111ebULL;
+        return value ^ (value >> 31);
+    }
+
 public:
-    explicit RandomGenerator(uint32_t seed = 42) : rng(seed), dist(0.0f, 1.0f) {}
-    val_t rand() { return dist(rng); }
+    explicit RandomGenerator(uint64_t startCounter) : counter(startCounter) {}
+
+    val_t rand() {
+        // Use the top 24 bits, exactly representable in a float, for a
+        // uniformly distributed value in [0, 1).
+        constexpr val_t INV_2_POW_24 = 1.0f / 16777216.0f;
+        return static_cast<val_t>(mix(counter++) >> 40) * INV_2_POW_24;
+    }
 };

+struct MpiContext {
+    int rank = 0;
+    int size = 1;
+
+    bool isRoot() const { return rank == 0; }
+};
+
+struct RowPartition {
+    size_t first = 0;
+    size_t count = 0;
+    std::vector<int> counts;
+    std::vector<int> displacements;
+
+    int ownerOf(size_t globalRow) const {
+        for (size_t rank = 0; rank < counts.size(); ++rank) {
+            const size_t firstRow = static_cast<size_t>(displacements[rank]);
+            const size_t rowCount = static_cast<size_t>(counts[rank]);
+            if (globalRow >= firstRow && globalRow < firstRow + rowCount) {
+                return static_cast<int>(rank);
+            }
+        }
+        return 0;
+    }
 };
 
+RowPartition makeRowPartition(size_t numRows, const MpiContext& mpi) {
+    RowPartition partition;
+    partition.counts.resize(static_cast<size_t>(mpi.size));
+    partition.displacements.resize(static_cast<size_t>(mpi.size));
+
+    const size_t processCount = static_cast<size_t>(mpi.size);
+    const size_t baseCount = numRows / processCount;
+    const size_t remainder = numRows % processCount;
+    size_t displacement = 0;
+    for (int rank = 0; rank < mpi.size; ++rank) {
+        const size_t rankCount = baseCount + (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+        partition.counts[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(rankCount);
+        partition.displacements[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(displacement);
+        if (rank == mpi.rank) {
+            partition.first = displacement;
+            partition.count = rankCount;
+        }
+        displacement += rankCount;
+    }
+    return partition;
+}

+// A counter-based generator makes samples independent of the rank that owns a
+// form-factor entry.  The counter is the position in the original row-major
+// sampling stream, so MPI process counts cannot change the random sequence.
 class RandomGenerator {
-    std::mt19937 rng;
-    std::uniform_real_distribution<val_t> dist;
+    uint64_t counter;

+    static uint64_t mix(uint64_t value) {
+        value += 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;
+        value = (value ^ (value >> 30)) * 0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9ULL;
+        value = (value ^ (value >> 27)) * 0x94d049bb133111ebULL;

+    explicit RandomGenerator(uint64_t startCounter) : counter(startCounter) {}
+
+    val_t rand() {
+        // Use the top 24 bits, exactly representable in a float, for a
+        // uniformly distributed value in [0, 1).
+        constexpr val_t INV_2_POW_24 = 1.0f / 16777216.0f;
+        return static_cast<val_t>(mix(counter++) >> 40) * INV_2_POW_24;

+struct MpiContext {
+    int rank = 0;
+    int size = 1;
+
+    bool isRoot() const { return rank == 0; }
+};
+
+struct RowPartition {
+    size_t first = 0;
+    size_t count = 0;
+    std::vector<int> counts;
+    std::vector<int> displacements;
+
+    int ownerOf(size_t globalRow) const {
+        for (size_t rank = 0; rank < counts.size(); ++rank) {
+            const size_t firstRow = static_cast<size_t>(displacements[rank]);
+            const size_t rowCount = static_cast<size_t>(counts[rank]);
+            if (globalRow >= firstRow && globalRow < firstRow + rowCount) {
+                return static_cast<int>(rank);
+            }
+        }
+        return 0;
+    }
 };
 
+RowPartition makeRowPartition(size_t numRows, const MpiContext& mpi) {
+    RowPartition partition;
+    partition.counts.resize(static_cast<size_t>(mpi.size));
+    partition.displacements.resize(static_cast<size_t>(mpi.size));
+
+    const size_t processCount = static_cast<size_t>(mpi.size);
+    const size_t baseCount = numRows / processCount;
+    const size_t remainder = numRows % processCount;
+    size_t displacement = 0;
+    for (int rank = 0; rank < mpi.size; ++rank) {
+        const size_t rankCount = baseCount + (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+        partition.counts[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(rankCount);
+        partition.displacements[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(displacement);
+        if (rank == mpi.rank) {
+            partition.first = displacement;
+            partition.count = rankCount;
+        }
+        displacement += rankCount;
+    }
+    return partition;
+}

+        // Use the top 24 bits, exactly representable in a float, for a
+        // uniformly distributed value in [0, 1).
+        constexpr val_t INV_2_POW_24 = 1.0f / 16777216.0f;
+        return static_cast<val_t>(mix(counter++) >> 40) * INV_2_POW_24;
+    }

+struct MpiContext {
+    int rank = 0;
+    int size = 1;
+
+    bool isRoot() const { return rank == 0; }
+};
+
+struct RowPartition {
+    size_t first = 0;
+    size_t count = 0;
+    std::vector<int> counts;
+    std::vector<int> displacements;
+
+    int ownerOf(size_t globalRow) const {
+        for (size_t rank = 0; rank < counts.size(); ++rank) {
+            const size_t firstRow = static_cast<size_t>(displacements[rank]);
+            const size_t rowCount = static_cast<size_t>(counts[rank]);
+            if (globalRow >= firstRow && globalRow < firstRow + rowCount) {
+                return static_cast<int>(rank);
+            }
+        }
+        return 0;
+    }
 };
 
+RowPartition makeRowPartition(size_t numRows, const MpiContext& mpi) {
+    RowPartition partition;
+    partition.counts.resize(static_cast<size_t>(mpi.size));
+    partition.displacements.resize(static_cast<size_t>(mpi.size));
+
+    const size_t processCount = static_cast<size_t>(mpi.size);
+    const size_t baseCount = numRows / processCount;
+    const size_t remainder = numRows % processCount;
+    size_t displacement = 0;
+    for (int rank = 0; rank < mpi.size; ++rank) {
+        const size_t rankCount = baseCount + (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+        partition.counts[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(rankCount);
+        partition.displacements[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(displacement);
+        if (rank == mpi.rank) {
+            partition.first = displacement;
+            partition.count = rankCount;
+        }
+        displacement += rankCount;
+    }
+    return partition;
+}

 // Random Number Generation
 // ============================================================================
 
+// A counter-based generator makes samples independent of the rank that owns a
+// form-factor entry.  The counter is the position in the original row-major
+// sampling stream, so MPI process counts cannot change the random sequence.
 class RandomGenerator {
-    std::mt19937 rng;
-    std::uniform_real_distribution<val_t> dist;
+    uint64_t counter;
+
+    static uint64_t mix(uint64_t value) {
+        value += 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;
+        value = (value ^ (value >> 30)) * 0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9ULL;
+        value = (value ^ (value >> 27)) * 0x94d049bb133111ebULL;
+        return value ^ (value >> 31);
+    }

-    explicit RandomGenerator(uint32_t seed = 42) : rng(seed), dist(0.0f, 1.0f) {}
-    val_t rand() { return dist(rng); }
+    explicit RandomGenerator(uint64_t startCounter) : counter(startCounter) {}
+
+    val_t rand() {
+        // Use the top 24 bits, exactly representable in a float, for a
+        // uniformly distributed value in [0, 1).
+        constexpr val_t INV_2_POW_24 = 1.0f / 16777216.0f;
+        return static_cast<val_t>(mix(counter++) >> 40) * INV_2_POW_24;
+    }

+struct MpiContext {
+    int rank = 0;
+    int size = 1;
+
+    bool isRoot() const { return rank == 0; }
+};
+
+struct RowPartition {
+    size_t first = 0;
+    size_t count = 0;
+    std::vector<int> counts;
+    std::vector<int> displacements;
+
+    int ownerOf(size_t globalRow) const {
+        for (size_t rank = 0; rank < counts.size(); ++rank) {
+            const size_t firstRow = static_cast<size_t>(displacements[rank]);
+            const size_t rowCount = static_cast<size_t>(counts[rank]);
+            if (globalRow >= firstRow && globalRow < firstRow + rowCount) {
+                return static_cast<int>(rank);
+            }
+        }
+        return 0;
+    }
 };
 
+RowPartition makeRowPartition(size_t numRows, const MpiContext& mpi) {
+    RowPartition partition;
+    partition.counts.resize(static_cast<size_t>(mpi.size));
+    partition.displacements.resize(static_cast<size_t>(mpi.size));
+
+    const size_t processCount = static_cast<size_t>(mpi.size);
+    const size_t baseCount = numRows / processCount;
+    const size_t remainder = numRows % processCount;
+    size_t displacement = 0;
+    for (int rank = 0; rank < mpi.size; ++rank) {
+        const size_t rankCount = baseCount + (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+        partition.counts[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(rankCount);
+        partition.displacements[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(displacement);
+        if (rank == mpi.rank) {
+            partition.first = displacement;
+            partition.count = rankCount;
+        }
+        displacement += rankCount;
+    }
+    return partition;
+}

+bool validateResults(const SimulationState& state, const MpiContext& mpi) {
+    uint64_t localReceivedEnergy = 0;

codex
One scalability refinement: I found an initialization pass that was unnecessarily repeated on every rank just to establish sample offsets. I’m replacing it with a distributed prefix sum, so those offsets are computed collectively from each rank’s local rows.
apply patch

 // Random Number Generation
 // ============================================================================
 
+// A counter-based generator makes samples independent of the rank that owns a
+// form-factor entry.  The counter is the position in the original row-major
+// sampling stream, so MPI process counts cannot change the random sequence.
 class RandomGenerator {
-    std::mt19937 rng;
-    std::uniform_real_distribution<val_t> dist;
+    uint64_t counter;
+
+    static uint64_t mix(uint64_t value) {
+        value += 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;
+        value = (value ^ (value >> 30)) * 0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9ULL;
+        value = (value ^ (value >> 27)) * 0x94d049bb133111ebULL;
+        return value ^ (value >> 31);
+    }
+
 public:
-    explicit RandomGenerator(uint32_t seed = 42) : rng(seed), dist(0.0f, 1.0f) {}
-    val_t rand() { return dist(rng); }
+    explicit RandomGenerator(uint64_t startCounter) : counter(startCounter) {}
+
+    val_t rand() {
+        // Use the top 24 bits, exactly representable in a float, for a
+        // uniformly distributed value in [0, 1).
+        constexpr val_t INV_2_POW_24 = 1.0f / 16777216.0f;
+        return static_cast<val_t>(mix(counter++) >> 40) * INV_2_POW_24;
+    }
 };

+struct MpiContext {
+    int rank = 0;
+    int size = 1;
+
+    bool isRoot() const { return rank == 0; }
 };
 
+struct RowPartition {
+    size_t first = 0;
+    size_t count = 0;
+    std::vector<int> counts;
+    std::vector<int> displacements;
+
+    int ownerOf(size_t globalRow) const {
+        for (size_t rank = 0; rank < counts.size(); ++rank) {
+            const size_t firstRow = static_cast<size_t>(displacements[rank]);
+            const size_t rowCount = static_cast<size_t>(counts[rank]);
+            if (globalRow >= firstRow && globalRow < firstRow + rowCount) {
+                return static_cast<int>(rank);
+            }
+        }
+        return 0;
+    }
+};
+
+RowPartition makeRowPartition(size_t numRows, const MpiContext& mpi) {
+    RowPartition partition;
+    partition.counts.resize(static_cast<size_t>(mpi.size));
+    partition.displacements.resize(static_cast<size_t>(mpi.size));
+
+    const size_t processCount = static_cast<size_t>(mpi.size);
+    const size_t baseCount = numRows / processCount;
+    const size_t remainder = numRows % processCount;
+    size_t displacement = 0;
+    for (int rank = 0; rank < mpi.size; ++rank) {
+        const size_t rankCount = baseCount + (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+        partition.counts[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(rankCount);
+        partition.displacements[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(displacement);
+        if (rank == mpi.rank) {
+            partition.first = displacement;
+            partition.count = rankCount;
+        }
+        displacement += rankCount;
+    }
+    return partition;
+}
+

 // Random Number Generation
 // ============================================================================
 
+// A counter-based generator makes samples independent of the rank that owns a
+// form-factor entry.  The counter is the position in the original row-major
+// sampling stream, so MPI process counts cannot change the random sequence.
 class RandomGenerator {
-    std::mt19937 rng;
-    std::uniform_real_distribution<val_t> dist;
+    uint64_t counter;
+
+    static uint64_t mix(uint64_t value) {
+        value += 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;
+        value = (value ^ (value >> 30)) * 0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9ULL;
+        value = (value ^ (value >> 27)) * 0x94d049bb133111ebULL;
+        return value ^ (value >> 31);
+    }
+
 public:
-    explicit RandomGenerator(uint32_t seed = 42) : rng(seed), dist(0.0f, 1.0f) {}
-    val_t rand() { return dist(rng); }
+    explicit RandomGenerator(uint64_t startCounter) : counter(startCounter) {}
+
+    val_t rand() {
+        // Use the top 24 bits, exactly representable in a float, for a
+        // uniformly distributed value in [0, 1).
+        constexpr val_t INV_2_POW_24 = 1.0f / 16777216.0f;
+        return static_cast<val_t>(mix(counter++) >> 40) * INV_2_POW_24;
+    }
 };

-    std::vector<val_t> radE;        // Emission radiosity (T x N matrix)
-    std::vector<val_t> radB;        // Reflected radiosity (T x N matrix)
-    std::vector<val_t> distances;   // Computed distances from source
+    std::vector<val_t> kij;         // Local rows of the N x N form-factor matrix
+    std::vector<int> tau;           // Local rows of the N x N delay matrix
+    std::vector<val_t> radB;        // T x local-triangle reflected radiosity
+    std::vector<val_t> localDistances;
+    std::vector<val_t> distances;   // Global distances, held only by rank zero
 
     Octree octree;                  // Spatial acceleration structure

-    size_t idx2d(size_t i, size_t j) const { return i * numTriangles + j; }
-    size_t idxTN(size_t t, size_t n) const { return t * numTriangles + n; }
+    size_t idx2d(size_t localI, size_t j) const { return localI * numTriangles + j; }
+    size_t idxTN(size_t t, size_t localI) const { return t * numLocalTriangles + localI; }
+};
+
+struct MpiContext {
+    int rank = 0;
+    int size = 1;
+
+    bool isRoot() const { return rank == 0; }
 };
 
+struct RowPartition {
+    size_t first = 0;
+    size_t count = 0;
+    std::vector<int> counts;
+    std::vector<int> displacements;
+
+    int ownerOf(size_t globalRow) const {
+        for (size_t rank = 0; rank < counts.size(); ++rank) {
+            const size_t firstRow = static_cast<size_t>(displacements[rank]);
+            const size_t rowCount = static_cast<size_t>(counts[rank]);
+            if (globalRow >= firstRow && globalRow < firstRow + rowCount) {
+                return static_cast<int>(rank);
+            }
+        }
+        return 0;
+    }
+};
+
+RowPartition makeRowPartition(size_t numRows, const MpiContext& mpi) {
+    RowPartition partition;
+    partition.counts.resize(static_cast<size_t>(mpi.size));
+    partition.displacements.resize(static_cast<size_t>(mpi.size));
+
+    const size_t processCount = static_cast<size_t>(mpi.size);
+    const size_t baseCount = numRows / processCount;
+    const size_t remainder = numRows % processCount;
+    size_t displacement = 0;
+    for (int rank = 0; rank < mpi.size; ++rank) {
+        const size_t rankCount = baseCount + (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+        partition.counts[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(rankCount);
+        partition.displacements[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(displacement);
+        if (rank == mpi.rank) {
+            partition.first = displacement;
+            partition.count = rankCount;
+        }
+        displacement += rankCount;
+    }
+    return partition;
+}
+

 // Random Number Generation
 // ============================================================================
 
+// A counter-based generator makes samples independent of the rank that owns a
+// form-factor entry.  The counter is the position in the original row-major
+// sampling stream, so MPI process counts cannot change the random sequence.
 class RandomGenerator {
-    std::mt19937 rng;
-    std::uniform_real_distribution<val_t> dist;
+    uint64_t counter;
+
+    static uint64_t mix(uint64_t value) {
+        value += 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;
+        value = (value ^ (value >> 30)) * 0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9ULL;
+        value = (value ^ (value >> 27)) * 0x94d049bb133111ebULL;
+        return value ^ (value >> 31);
+    }
+

-    explicit RandomGenerator(uint32_t seed = 42) : rng(seed), dist(0.0f, 1.0f) {}
-    val_t rand() { return dist(rng); }
+    explicit RandomGenerator(uint64_t startCounter) : counter(startCounter) {}
+
+    val_t rand() {
+        // Use the top 24 bits, exactly representable in a float, for a
+        // uniformly distributed value in [0, 1).
+        constexpr val_t INV_2_POW_24 = 1.0f / 16777216.0f;
+        return static_cast<val_t>(mix(counter++) >> 40) * INV_2_POW_24;
+    }

+struct MpiContext {
+    int rank = 0;
+    int size = 1;
+
+    bool isRoot() const { return rank == 0; }
 };
 
+struct RowPartition {
+    size_t first = 0;
+    size_t count = 0;
+    std::vector<int> counts;
+    std::vector<int> displacements;
+
+    int ownerOf(size_t globalRow) const {
+        for (size_t rank = 0; rank < counts.size(); ++rank) {
+            const size_t firstRow = static_cast<size_t>(displacements[rank]);
+            const size_t rowCount = static_cast<size_t>(counts[rank]);
+            if (globalRow >= firstRow && globalRow < firstRow + rowCount) {
+                return static_cast<int>(rank);
+            }
+        }
+        return 0;
+    }
+};
+
+RowPartition makeRowPartition(size_t numRows, const MpiContext& mpi) {
+    RowPartition partition;
+    partition.counts.resize(static_cast<size_t>(mpi.size));
+    partition.displacements.resize(static_cast<size_t>(mpi.size));
+
+    const size_t processCount = static_cast<size_t>(mpi.size);
+    const size_t baseCount = numRows / processCount;
+    const size_t remainder = numRows % processCount;
+    size_t displacement = 0;
+    for (int rank = 0; rank < mpi.size; ++rank) {
+        const size_t rankCount = baseCount + (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+        partition.counts[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(rankCount);
+        partition.displacements[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(displacement);
+        if (rank == mpi.rank) {
+            partition.first = displacement;
+            partition.count = rankCount;
+        }
+        displacement += rankCount;
+    }
+    return partition;
+}

+bool validateResults(const SimulationState& state, const MpiContext& mpi) {

 // Random Number Generation
 // ============================================================================
 
+// A counter-based generator makes samples independent of the rank that owns a
+// form-factor entry.  The counter is the position in the original row-major
+// sampling stream, so MPI process counts cannot change the random sequence.
 class RandomGenerator {
-    std::mt19937 rng;
-    std::uniform_real_distribution<val_t> dist;
+    uint64_t counter;
+
+    static uint64_t mix(uint64_t value) {
+        value += 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;
+        value = (value ^ (value >> 30)) * 0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9ULL;
+        value = (value ^ (value >> 27)) * 0x94d049bb133111ebULL;
+        return value ^ (value >> 31);
+    }
+
 public:
-    explicit RandomGenerator(uint32_t seed = 42) : rng(seed), dist(0.0f, 1.0f) {}
-    val_t rand() { return dist(rng); }
+    explicit RandomGenerator(uint64_t startCounter) : counter(startCounter) {}
+
+    val_t rand() {
+        // Use the top 24 bits, exactly representable in a float, for a
+        // uniformly distributed value in [0, 1).
+        constexpr val_t INV_2_POW_24 = 1.0f / 16777216.0f;
+        return static_cast<val_t>(mix(counter++) >> 40) * INV_2_POW_24;
+    }
 };

+struct MpiContext {
+    int rank = 0;
+    int size = 1;
+
+    bool isRoot() const { return rank == 0; }
 };
 
+struct RowPartition {
+    size_t first = 0;
+    size_t count = 0;
+    std::vector<int> counts;
+    std::vector<int> displacements;
+
+    int ownerOf(size_t globalRow) const {
+        for (size_t rank = 0; rank < counts.size(); ++rank) {
+            const size_t firstRow = static_cast<size_t>(displacements[rank]);
+            const size_t rowCount = static_cast<size_t>(counts[rank]);
+            if (globalRow >= firstRow && globalRow < firstRow + rowCount) {
+                return static_cast<int>(rank);
+            }
+        }
+        return 0;
+    }
+};
+
+RowPartition makeRowPartition(size_t numRows, const MpiContext& mpi) {
+    RowPartition partition;
+    partition.counts.resize(static_cast<size_t>(mpi.size));
+    partition.displacements.resize(static_cast<size_t>(mpi.size));
+
+    const size_t processCount = static_cast<size_t>(mpi.size);
+    const size_t baseCount = numRows / processCount;
+    const size_t remainder = numRows % processCount;
+    size_t displacement = 0;
+    for (int rank = 0; rank < mpi.size; ++rank) {
+        const size_t rankCount = baseCount + (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+        partition.counts[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(rankCount);
+        partition.displacements[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(displacement);
+        if (rank == mpi.rank) {
+            partition.first = displacement;
+            partition.count = rankCount;
+        }
+        displacement += rankCount;
+    }
+    return partition;
+}
+

+void computeFormFactors(SimulationState& state, const MpiContext& mpi) {
+    if (mpi.isRoot()) {
+        printf("Computing form factors (Kij) across %d MPI ranks...\n", mpi.size);
+    }
+
+    // Determine this contiguous row block's place in the conceptual serial
+    // random stream.  Exscan avoids an O(N^2) replicated setup pass.
+    uint64_t localDrawCount = 0;
+    for (size_t localI = 0; localI < state.numLocalTriangles; ++localI) {
+        const size_t i = state.firstLocalTriangle + localI;
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {
+            if (i != j && state.triangles[i].normal().dot(state.triangles[j].normal()) <= 0.99f) {
+                localDrawCount += 2 * NUM_RAYS;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    uint64_t randomDraw = 0;
+    MPI_Exscan(&localDrawCount, &randomDraw, 1, MPI_UINT64_T, MPI_SUM, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    if (mpi.rank == 0) randomDraw = 0;

 // Random Number Generation
 // ============================================================================
 
+// A counter-based generator makes samples independent of the rank that owns a
+// form-factor entry.  The counter is the position in the original row-major
+// sampling stream, so MPI process counts cannot change the random sequence.
 class RandomGenerator {
-    std::mt19937 rng;
-    std::uniform_real_distribution<val_t> dist;
+    uint64_t counter;
+
+    static uint64_t mix(uint64_t value) {
+        value += 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;
+        value = (value ^ (value >> 30)) * 0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9ULL;
+        value = (value ^ (value >> 27)) * 0x94d049bb133111ebULL;
+        return value ^ (value >> 31);
+    }
+
 public:
-    explicit RandomGenerator(uint32_t seed = 42) : rng(seed), dist(0.0f, 1.0f) {}
-    val_t rand() { return dist(rng); }
+    explicit RandomGenerator(uint64_t startCounter) : counter(startCounter) {}
+
+    val_t rand() {
+        // Use the top 24 bits, exactly representable in a float, for a
+        // uniformly distributed value in [0, 1).
+        constexpr val_t INV_2_POW_24 = 1.0f / 16777216.0f;
+        return static_cast<val_t>(mix(counter++) >> 40) * INV_2_POW_24;
+    }
 };

-    std::vector<val_t> kij;         // Form factors (N x N matrix, row-major)
-    std::vector<int> tau;           // Time delays (N x N matrix, row-major)
-    std::vector<val_t> radE;        // Emission radiosity (T x N matrix)
-    std::vector<val_t> radB;        // Reflected radiosity (T x N matrix)
-    std::vector<val_t> distances;   // Computed distances from source
+    std::vector<val_t> kij;         // Local rows of the N x N form-factor matrix
+    std::vector<int> tau;           // Local rows of the N x N delay matrix
+    std::vector<val_t> radB;        // T x local-triangle reflected radiosity
+    std::vector<val_t> localDistances;
+    std::vector<val_t> distances;   // Global distances, held only by rank zero
 
     Octree octree;                  // Spatial acceleration structure

-    size_t idx2d(size_t i, size_t j) const { return i * numTriangles + j; }
-    size_t idxTN(size_t t, size_t n) const { return t * numTriangles + n; }
+    size_t idx2d(size_t localI, size_t j) const { return localI * numTriangles + j; }
+    size_t idxTN(size_t t, size_t localI) const { return t * numLocalTriangles + localI; }
+};
+
+struct MpiContext {
+    int rank = 0;
+    int size = 1;
+
+    bool isRoot() const { return rank == 0; }
 };
 
+struct RowPartition {
+    size_t first = 0;
+    size_t count = 0;
+    std::vector<int> counts;
+    std::vector<int> displacements;
+
+    int ownerOf(size_t globalRow) const {
+        for (size_t rank = 0; rank < counts.size(); ++rank) {
+            const size_t firstRow = static_cast<size_t>(displacements[rank]);
+            const size_t rowCount = static_cast<size_t>(counts[rank]);
+            if (globalRow >= firstRow && globalRow < firstRow + rowCount) {
+                return static_cast<int>(rank);
+            }
+        }
+        return 0;
+    }
+};
+
+RowPartition makeRowPartition(size_t numRows, const MpiContext& mpi) {
+    RowPartition partition;
+    partition.counts.resize(static_cast<size_t>(mpi.size));
+    partition.displacements.resize(static_cast<size_t>(mpi.size));
+
+    const size_t processCount = static_cast<size_t>(mpi.size);
+    const size_t baseCount = numRows / processCount;
+    const size_t remainder = numRows % processCount;
+    size_t displacement = 0;
+    for (int rank = 0; rank < mpi.size; ++rank) {
+        const size_t rankCount = baseCount + (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+        partition.counts[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(rankCount);
+        partition.displacements[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(displacement);
+        if (rank == mpi.rank) {
+            partition.first = displacement;
+            partition.count = rankCount;
+        }
+        displacement += rankCount;
+    }
+    return partition;
+}
+

 // Random Number Generation
 // ============================================================================
 
+// A counter-based generator makes samples independent of the rank that owns a
+// form-factor entry.  The counter is the position in the original row-major
+// sampling stream, so MPI process counts cannot change the random sequence.
 class RandomGenerator {
-    std::mt19937 rng;
-    std::uniform_real_distribution<val_t> dist;
+    uint64_t counter;
+
+    static uint64_t mix(uint64_t value) {
+        value += 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;
+        value = (value ^ (value >> 30)) * 0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9ULL;
+        value = (value ^ (value >> 27)) * 0x94d049bb133111ebULL;
+        return value ^ (value >> 31);
+    }
+
 public:
-    explicit RandomGenerator(uint32_t seed = 42) : rng(seed), dist(0.0f, 1.0f) {}
-    val_t rand() { return dist(rng); }
+    explicit RandomGenerator(uint64_t startCounter) : counter(startCounter) {}
+
+    val_t rand() {
+        // Use the top 24 bits, exactly representable in a float, for a
+        // uniformly distributed value in [0, 1).
+        constexpr val_t INV_2_POW_24 = 1.0f / 16777216.0f;
+        return static_cast<val_t>(mix(counter++) >> 40) * INV_2_POW_24;
+    }
 };

+struct MpiContext {
+    int rank = 0;
+    int size = 1;
+
+    bool isRoot() const { return rank == 0; }
 };
 
+struct RowPartition {
+    size_t first = 0;
+    size_t count = 0;
+    std::vector<int> counts;
+    std::vector<int> displacements;
+
+    int ownerOf(size_t globalRow) const {
+        for (size_t rank = 0; rank < counts.size(); ++rank) {
+            const size_t firstRow = static_cast<size_t>(displacements[rank]);
+            const size_t rowCount = static_cast<size_t>(counts[rank]);
+            if (globalRow >= firstRow && globalRow < firstRow + rowCount) {
+                return static_cast<int>(rank);
+            }
+        }
+        return 0;
+    }
+};
+
+RowPartition makeRowPartition(size_t numRows, const MpiContext& mpi) {
+    RowPartition partition;
+    partition.counts.resize(static_cast<size_t>(mpi.size));
+    partition.displacements.resize(static_cast<size_t>(mpi.size));
+
+    const size_t processCount = static_cast<size_t>(mpi.size);
+    const size_t baseCount = numRows / processCount;
+    const size_t remainder = numRows % processCount;
+    size_t displacement = 0;
+    for (int rank = 0; rank < mpi.size; ++rank) {
+        const size_t rankCount = baseCount + (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+        partition.counts[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(rankCount);
+        partition.displacements[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(displacement);
+        if (rank == mpi.rank) {
+            partition.first = displacement;
+            partition.count = rankCount;
+        }
+        displacement += rankCount;
+    }
+    return partition;
+}

 // Random Number Generation
 // ============================================================================
 
+// A counter-based generator makes samples independent of the rank that owns a
+// form-factor entry.  The counter is the position in the original row-major
+// sampling stream, so MPI process counts cannot change the random sequence.
 class RandomGenerator {
-    std::mt19937 rng;
-    std::uniform_real_distribution<val_t> dist;
+    uint64_t counter;
+
+    static uint64_t mix(uint64_t value) {
+        value += 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;
+        value = (value ^ (value >> 30)) * 0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9ULL;
+        value = (value ^ (value >> 27)) * 0x94d049bb133111ebULL;
+        return value ^ (value >> 31);
+    }
+
 public:
-    explicit RandomGenerator(uint32_t seed = 42) : rng(seed), dist(0.0f, 1.0f) {}
-    val_t rand() { return dist(rng); }
+    explicit RandomGenerator(uint64_t startCounter) : counter(startCounter) {}
+
+    val_t rand() {
+        // Use the top 24 bits, exactly representable in a float, for a
+        // uniformly distributed value in [0, 1).
+        constexpr val_t INV_2_POW_24 = 1.0f / 16777216.0f;
+        return static_cast<val_t>(mix(counter++) >> 40) * INV_2_POW_24;
+    }
 };

+
+struct MpiContext {
+    int rank = 0;
+    int size = 1;
+
+    bool isRoot() const { return rank == 0; }
 };
 
+struct RowPartition {
+    size_t first = 0;
+    size_t count = 0;
+    std::vector<int> counts;
+    std::vector<int> displacements;
+
+    int ownerOf(size_t globalRow) const {
+        for (size_t rank = 0; rank < counts.size(); ++rank) {
+            const size_t firstRow = static_cast<size_t>(displacements[rank]);
+            const size_t rowCount = static_cast<size_t>(counts[rank]);
+            if (globalRow >= firstRow && globalRow < firstRow + rowCount) {
+                return static_cast<int>(rank);
+            }
+        }
+        return 0;
+    }
+};
+
+RowPartition makeRowPartition(size_t numRows, const MpiContext& mpi) {
+    RowPartition partition;
+    partition.counts.resize(static_cast<size_t>(mpi.size));
+    partition.displacements.resize(static_cast<size_t>(mpi.size));
+
+    const size_t processCount = static_cast<size_t>(mpi.size);
+    const size_t baseCount = numRows / processCount;
+    const size_t remainder = numRows % processCount;
+    size_t displacement = 0;
+    for (int rank = 0; rank < mpi.size; ++rank) {
+        const size_t rankCount = baseCount + (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+        partition.counts[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(rankCount);
+        partition.displacements[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(displacement);
+        if (rank == mpi.rank) {
+            partition.first = displacement;
+            partition.count = rankCount;
+        }
+        displacement += rankCount;
+    }
+    return partition;
+}
+

 // ============================================================================

-void computeFormFactors(SimulationState& state) {
-    printf("Computing form factors (Kij)...\n");
-    RandomGenerator rng(42);
+void computeFormFactors(SimulationState& state, const MpiContext& mpi) {
+    if (mpi.isRoot()) {
+        printf("Computing form factors (Kij) across %d MPI ranks...\n", mpi.size);
+    }
+
+    // Determine this contiguous row block's place in the conceptual serial
+    // random stream.  Exscan avoids an O(N^2) replicated setup pass.
+    uint64_t localDrawCount = 0;
+    for (size_t localI = 0; localI < state.numLocalTriangles; ++localI) {
+        const size_t i = state.firstLocalTriangle + localI;
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {
+            if (i != j && state.triangles[i].normal().dot(state.triangles[j].normal()) <= 0.99f) {
+                localDrawCount += 2 * NUM_RAYS;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+

codex
The distributed prefix-sum refinement is complete. The final design has no rank-zero compute bottleneck: each rank computes its own rows, timestep exchanges are collective, and only reporting data is gathered to rank zero.

 // Random Number Generation
 // ============================================================================
 
+// A counter-based generator makes samples independent of the rank that owns a
+// form-factor entry.  The counter is the position in the original row-major
+// sampling stream, so MPI process counts cannot change the random sequence.
 class RandomGenerator {
-    std::mt19937 rng;
-    std::uniform_real_distribution<val_t> dist;
+    uint64_t counter;
+
+    static uint64_t mix(uint64_t value) {
+        value += 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;
+        value = (value ^ (value >> 30)) * 0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9ULL;
+        value = (value ^ (value >> 27)) * 0x94d049bb133111ebULL;
+        return value ^ (value >> 31);
+    }
+
 public:
-    explicit RandomGenerator(uint32_t seed = 42) : rng(seed), dist(0.0f, 1.0f) {}
-    val_t rand() { return dist(rng); }
+    explicit RandomGenerator(uint64_t startCounter) : counter(startCounter) {}
+
+    val_t rand() {
+        // Use the top 24 bits, exactly representable in a float, for a
+        // uniformly distributed value in [0, 1).
+        constexpr val_t INV_2_POW_24 = 1.0f / 16777216.0f;
+        return static_cast<val_t>(mix(counter++) >> 40) * INV_2_POW_24;
+    }
 };

-    std::vector<val_t> radE;        // Emission radiosity (T x N matrix)
-    std::vector<val_t> radB;        // Reflected radiosity (T x N matrix)
-    std::vector<val_t> distances;   // Computed distances from source
+    std::vector<val_t> kij;         // Local rows of the N x N form-factor matrix
+    std::vector<int> tau;           // Local rows of the N x N delay matrix
+    std::vector<val_t> radB;        // T x local-triangle reflected radiosity

+    std::vector<val_t> distances;   // Global distances, held only by rank zero
 
     Octree octree;                  // Spatial acceleration structure

-    size_t idx2d(size_t i, size_t j) const { return i * numTriangles + j; }
-    size_t idxTN(size_t t, size_t n) const { return t * numTriangles + n; }
+    size_t idx2d(size_t localI, size_t j) const { return localI * numTriangles + j; }
+    size_t idxTN(size_t t, size_t localI) const { return t * numLocalTriangles + localI; }
+};
+
+struct MpiContext {
+    int rank = 0;
+    int size = 1;
+
+    bool isRoot() const { return rank == 0; }
 };
 
+struct RowPartition {
+    size_t first = 0;
+    size_t count = 0;
+    std::vector<int> counts;
+    std::vector<int> displacements;
+
+    int ownerOf(size_t globalRow) const {
+        for (size_t rank = 0; rank < counts.size(); ++rank) {
+            const size_t firstRow = static_cast<size_t>(displacements[rank]);
+            const size_t rowCount = static_cast<size_t>(counts[rank]);
+            if (globalRow >= firstRow && globalRow < firstRow + rowCount) {
+                return static_cast<int>(rank);
+            }
+        }
+        return 0;
+    }
+};
+
+RowPartition makeRowPartition(size_t numRows, const MpiContext& mpi) {
+    RowPartition partition;
+    partition.counts.resize(static_cast<size_t>(mpi.size));
+    partition.displacements.resize(static_cast<size_t>(mpi.size));
+
+    const size_t processCount = static_cast<size_t>(mpi.size);
+    const size_t baseCount = numRows / processCount;
+    const size_t remainder = numRows % processCount;
+    size_t displacement = 0;
+    for (int rank = 0; rank < mpi.size; ++rank) {
+        const size_t rankCount = baseCount + (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+        partition.counts[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(rankCount);
+        partition.displacements[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(displacement);
+        if (rank == mpi.rank) {
+            partition.first = displacement;
+            partition.count = rankCount;
+        }
+        displacement += rankCount;
+    }
+    return partition;
+}

   561	
   562	struct MpiContext {
   563	    int rank = 0;
   564	    int size = 1;
   565	
   566	    bool isRoot() const { return rank == 0; }
   567	};
   568	
   569	struct RowPartition {
   570	    size_t first = 0;
   571	    size_t count = 0;
   572	    std::vector<int> counts;
   573	    std::vector<int> displacements;
   574	
   575	    int ownerOf(size_t globalRow) const {
   576	        for (size_t rank = 0; rank < counts.size(); ++rank) {
   577	            const size_t firstRow = static_cast<size_t>(displacements[rank]);
   578	            const size_t rowCount = static_cast<size_t>(counts[rank]);
   579	            if (globalRow >= firstRow && globalRow < firstRow + rowCount) {
   580	                return static_cast<int>(rank);
   581	            }
   582	        }
   583	        return 0;
   584	    }
   585	};
   586	
   587	RowPartition makeRowPartition(size_t numRows, const MpiContext& mpi) {
   588	    RowPartition partition;
   589	    partition.counts.resize(static_cast<size_t>(mpi.size));
   590	    partition.displacements.resize(static_cast<size_t>(mpi.size));
   591	
   592	    const size_t processCount = static_cast<size_t>(mpi.size);
   593	    const size_t baseCount = numRows / processCount;
   594	    const size_t remainder = numRows % processCount;
   595	    size_t displacement = 0;
   596	    for (int rank = 0; rank < mpi.size; ++rank) {
   597	        const size_t rankCount = baseCount + (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
   598	        partition.counts[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(rankCount);
   599	        partition.displacements[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(displacement);
   600	        if (rank == mpi.rank) {
   601	            partition.first = displacement;
   602	            partition.count = rankCount;
   603	        }
   604	        displacement += rankCount;
   605	    }
   606	    return partition;
   607	}
   608	
   609	// ============================================================================
   610	// Initialization
   611	// ============================================================================
   612	
   613	void initializeSimulation(SimulationState& state, int subdivisions, size_t timesteps,
   614	                          size_t sourceIdx, val_t reflectivity,
   615	                          const RowPartition& partition, const MpiContext& mpi) {
   616	    // Generate mesh
   617	    IcosphereMesh mesh(subdivisions, 10.0f);  // Radius 10 units
   618	    state.triangles = std::move(mesh.triangles);
   619	    state.numTriangles = state.triangles.size();
   620	    state.numTimesteps = timesteps;
   621	    state.sourceIndex = sourceIdx % state.numTriangles;
   622	
   623	    if (mpi.isRoot()) {
   624	        printf("Generated icosphere mesh with %zu triangles\n", state.numTriangles);
   625	    }
   626	
   627	    // Build octree for spatial acceleration
   628	    if (mpi.isRoot()) {
   629	        printf("Building octree on every MPI rank...\n");
   630	    }
   631	    state.octree.build(state.triangles);
   632	
   633	    // Initialize areas
   634	    state.areas.resize(state.numTriangles);
   635	    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {
   636	        state.areas[i] = state.triangles[i].area();
   637	    }
   638	
   639	    // Initialize reflectivity
   640	    state.rho.resize(state.numTriangles, reflectivity);
   641	
   642	    state.firstLocalTriangle = partition.first;
   643	    state.numLocalTriangles = partition.count;
   644	
   645	    // Matrix rows and radiosity traces are distributed by receiving triangle.
   646	    state.kij.assign(state.numLocalTriangles * state.numTriangles, ZERO);
   647	    state.tau.assign(state.numLocalTriangles * state.numTriangles, 0);
   648	    state.radB.assign(timesteps * state.numLocalTriangles, ZERO);
   649	    state.localDistances.assign(state.numLocalTriangles, ZERO);
   650	
   651	}
   652	
   653	// ============================================================================
   654	// Precomputation Phase
   655	// ============================================================================
   656	
   657	void computeFormFactors(SimulationState& state, const MpiContext& mpi) {
   658	    if (mpi.isRoot()) {
   659	        printf("Computing form factors (Kij) across %d MPI ranks...\n", mpi.size);
   660	    }
   661	
   662	    // Determine this contiguous row block's place in the conceptual serial
   663	    // random stream.  Exscan avoids an O(N^2) replicated setup pass.
   664	    uint64_t localDrawCount = 0;
   665	    for (size_t localI = 0; localI < state.numLocalTriangles; ++localI) {
   666	        const size_t i = state.firstLocalTriangle + localI;
   667	        for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {
   668	            if (i != j && state.triangles[i].normal().dot(state.triangles[j].normal()) <= 0.99f) {
   669	                localDrawCount += 2 * NUM_RAYS;
   670	            }
   671	        }
   672	    }
   673	
   674	    uint64_t randomDraw = 0;
   675	    MPI_Exscan(&localDrawCount, &randomDraw, 1, MPI_UINT64_T, MPI_SUM, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   676	    if (mpi.rank == 0) randomDraw = 0;
   677	
   678	    for (size_t localI = 0; localI < state.numLocalTriangles; ++localI) {
   679	        const size_t i = state.firstLocalTriangle + localI;
   680	        for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {
   681	            if (i == j) continue;
   682	
   683	            // A culled pair consumes no samples, matching computeKij.
   684	            if (state.triangles[i].normal().dot(state.triangles[j].normal()) > 0.99f) continue;
   685	
   686	            RandomGenerator rng(randomDraw);
   687	            state.kij[state.idx2d(localI, j)] = computeKij(
   688	                i, j, state.triangles, state.octree, rng);
   689	            randomDraw += 2 * NUM_RAYS;
   690	        }
   691	    }
   692	}
   693	
   694	int computeTimeDelays(SimulationState& state, const MpiContext& mpi) {
   695	    if (mpi.isRoot()) {
   696	        printf("Computing time delays (Tau) across %d MPI ranks...\n", mpi.size);
   697	    }
   698	
   699	    int localMaxTau = 0;
   700	    for (size_t localI = 0; localI < state.numLocalTriangles; ++localI) {
   701	        const size_t i = state.firstLocalTriangle + localI;
   702	        for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {
   703	            if (i == j) continue;
   704	            const int tau = computeTau(state.triangles[i], state.triangles[j]);
   705	            state.tau[state.idx2d(localI, j)] = tau;
   706	            localMaxTau = std::max(localMaxTau, tau);
   707	        }
   708	    }
   709	
   710	    int maxTau = 0;
   711	    MPI_Allreduce(&localMaxTau, &maxTau, 1, MPI_INT, MPI_MAX, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   712	    return maxTau;
   713	}
   714	
   715	// ============================================================================
   716	// Simulation Phase (Wave Propagation)
   717	// ============================================================================
   718	
   719	void runSimulation(SimulationState& state, int maxTau, const RowPartition& partition,
   720	                   const MpiContext& mpi) {
   721	    if (mpi.isRoot()) {
   722	        printf("Running wave propagation simulation across %d MPI ranks...\n", mpi.size);
   723	    }
   724	
   725	    // A non-self triangle always has a positive delay.  Therefore the current
   726	    // timestep can overwrite the oldest slot after it has been consumed.
   727	    const size_t historyDepth = static_cast<size_t>(std::max(1, maxTau));
   728	    std::vector<val_t> history(historyDepth * state.numTriangles, ZERO);
   729	    const size_t timeOff = state.numTimesteps / 2;
   730	
   731	    for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {
   732	        for (size_t localI = 0; localI < state.numLocalTriangles; ++localI) {
   733	            const size_t i = state.firstLocalTriangle + localI;
   734	            val_t sumB = ZERO;
   735	            const val_t* const kijRow = &state.kij[state.idx2d(localI, 0)];
   736	            const int* const tauRow = &state.tau[state.idx2d(localI, 0)];
   737	
   738	            for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {
   739	                if (i == j) continue;
   740	
   741	                const int tauij = tauRow[j];
   742	
   743	                // Skip if wave hasn't yet propagated from j to i
   744	                if (static_cast<int>(t) < tauij) continue;
   745	
   746	                const val_t kij = kijRow[j];
   747	                if (kij <= ZERO) continue;
   748	
   749	                // Get radiosity from source triangle at time when emission occurred
   750	                const size_t srcTime = t - static_cast<size_t>(tauij);
   751	                const size_t historySlot = srcTime % historyDepth;
   752	                const val_t radJ = history[historySlot * state.numTriangles + j];
   753	                if (radJ <= ZERO) continue;
   754	
   755	                // Accumulate contribution: form factor * area * source radiosity
   756	                sumB += std::min(kij * state.areas[j], ONE) * radJ;
   757	            }
   758	
   759	            // Update radiosity: reflection + emission
   760	            const val_t emission = (i == state.sourceIndex && t < timeOff) ? ONE : ZERO;
   761	            state.radB[state.idxTN(t, localI)] = state.rho[i] * sumB + emission;
   762	        }
   763	
   764	        val_t* localRadiosity = state.numLocalTriangles == 0
   765	            ? nullptr : state.radB.data() + state.idxTN(t, 0);
   766	        MPI_Allgatherv(localRadiosity, static_cast<int>(state.numLocalTriangles),
   767	                       MPI_FLOAT, &history[(t % historyDepth) * state.numTriangles],
   768	                       partition.counts.data(), partition.displacements.data(), MPI_FLOAT,
   769	                       MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   770	    }
   771	}
   772	
   773	// ============================================================================
   774	// Distance Computation (Cross-Correlation)
   775	// ============================================================================
   776	
   777	void computeDistances(SimulationState& state, const RowPartition& partition,
   778	                      const MpiContext& mpi) {
   779	    if (mpi.isRoot()) {
   780	        printf("Computing distances via cross-correlation across %d MPI ranks...\n", mpi.size);
   781	    }
   782	
   783	    std::vector<val_t> sourceTrace(state.numTimesteps, ZERO);
   784	    const int sourceOwner = partition.ownerOf(state.sourceIndex);
   785	    if (mpi.rank == sourceOwner) {
   786	        const size_t localSource = state.sourceIndex - state.firstLocalTriangle;
   787	        for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {
   788	            sourceTrace[t] = state.radB[state.idxTN(t, localSource)];
   789	        }
   790	    }
   791	    MPI_Bcast(sourceTrace.data(), static_cast<int>(state.numTimesteps), MPI_FLOAT,
   792	              sourceOwner, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   793	
   794	    for (size_t localI = 0; localI < state.numLocalTriangles; ++localI) {
   795	        val_t maxCorr = ZERO;
   796	        int bestT = 0;
   797	
   798	        // Discrete cross-correlation to find time delay
   799	        for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {
   800	            val_t sum = ZERO;
   801	
   802	            for (size_t tt = t; tt < state.numTimesteps; ++tt) {
   803	                const val_t pB = state.radB[state.idxTN(tt, localI)];
   804	                const val_t pS = sourceTrace[tt - t];
   805	                sum += pS * pB;
   806	            }
   807	
   808	            if (sum > maxCorr) {
   809	                maxCorr = sum;
   810	                bestT = static_cast<int>(t);
   811	            }
   812	        }
   813	
   814	        state.localDistances[localI] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(bestT);
   815	    }
   816	}
   817	
   818	// ============================================================================
   819	// Validation
   820	// ============================================================================
   821	
   822	void gatherDistances(SimulationState& state, const RowPartition& partition,
   823	                     const MpiContext& mpi) {
   824	    if (mpi.isRoot()) {
   825	        state.distances.resize(state.numTriangles);
   826	    }
   827	    const val_t* sendBuffer = state.numLocalTriangles == 0 ? nullptr : state.localDistances.data();
   828	    val_t* receiveBuffer = mpi.isRoot() ? state.distances.data() : nullptr;
   829	    MPI_Gatherv(sendBuffer, static_cast<int>(state.numLocalTriangles), MPI_FLOAT,
   830	                receiveBuffer, partition.counts.data(), partition.displacements.data(), MPI_FLOAT,
   831	                0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   832	}
   833	
   834	bool validateResults(const SimulationState& state, const MpiContext& mpi) {
   835	    uint64_t localReceivedEnergy = 0;
   836	    for (size_t localI = 0; localI < state.numLocalTriangles; ++localI) {
   837	        for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {
   838	            if (state.radB[state.idxTN(t, localI)] > EPSILON) {
   839	                ++localReceivedEnergy;
   840	                break;
   841	            }
   842	        }
   843	    }
   844	
   845	    uint64_t localNonZeroKij = 0;
   846	    for (val_t value : state.kij) {
   847	        if (value > EPSILON) ++localNonZeroKij;
   848	    }
   849	
   850	    uint64_t receivedEnergy = 0;
   851	    uint64_t nonZeroKij = 0;
   852	    MPI_Reduce(&localReceivedEnergy, &receivedEnergy, 1, MPI_UINT64_T, MPI_SUM, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   853	    MPI_Reduce(&localNonZeroKij, &nonZeroKij, 1, MPI_UINT64_T, MPI_SUM, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   854	
   855	    int passed = 1;
   856	    if (!mpi.isRoot()) {
   857	        MPI_Bcast(&passed, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   858	        return passed != 0;
   859	    }
   860

  1001	            if (mpi.isRoot()) printUsage(argv[0]);
  1002	            MPI_Finalize();

 // Random Number Generation
 // ============================================================================
 
+// A counter-based generator makes samples independent of the rank that owns a
+// form-factor entry.  The counter is the position in the original row-major
+// sampling stream, so MPI process counts cannot change the random sequence.
 class RandomGenerator {
-    std::mt19937 rng;
-    std::uniform_real_distribution<val_t> dist;
+    uint64_t counter;
+
+    static uint64_t mix(uint64_t value) {
+        value += 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;
+        value = (value ^ (value >> 30)) * 0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9ULL;
+        value = (value ^ (value >> 27)) * 0x94d049bb133111ebULL;
+        return value ^ (value >> 31);
+    }
+
 public:
-    explicit RandomGenerator(uint32_t seed = 42) : rng(seed), dist(0.0f, 1.0f) {}
-    val_t rand() { return dist(rng); }
+    explicit RandomGenerator(uint64_t startCounter) : counter(startCounter) {}
+
+    val_t rand() {
+        // Use the top 24 bits, exactly representable in a float, for a
+        // uniformly distributed value in [0, 1).
+        constexpr val_t INV_2_POW_24 = 1.0f / 16777216.0f;
+        return static_cast<val_t>(mix(counter++) >> 40) * INV_2_POW_24;
+    }
 };

+    size_t idxTN(size_t t, size_t localI) const { return t * numLocalTriangles + localI; }
+};
+
+struct MpiContext {
+    int rank = 0;
+    int size = 1;
+
+    bool isRoot() const { return rank == 0; }
 };
 
+struct RowPartition {
+    size_t first = 0;
+    size_t count = 0;
+    std::vector<int> counts;
+    std::vector<int> displacements;
+
+    int ownerOf(size_t globalRow) const {
+        for (size_t rank = 0; rank < counts.size(); ++rank) {
+            const size_t firstRow = static_cast<size_t>(displacements[rank]);
+            const size_t rowCount = static_cast<size_t>(counts[rank]);
+            if (globalRow >= firstRow && globalRow < firstRow + rowCount) {
+                return static_cast<int>(rank);
+            }
+        }
+        return 0;
+    }
+};
+
+RowPartition makeRowPartition(size_t numRows, const MpiContext& mpi) {
+    RowPartition partition;
+    partition.counts.resize(static_cast<size_t>(mpi.size));
+    partition.displacements.resize(static_cast<size_t>(mpi.size));
+
+    const size_t processCount = static_cast<size_t>(mpi.size);
+    const size_t baseCount = numRows / processCount;
+    const size_t remainder = numRows % processCount;
+    size_t displacement = 0;
+    for (int rank = 0; rank < mpi.size; ++rank) {
+        const size_t rankCount = baseCount + (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+        partition.counts[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(rankCount);
+        partition.displacements[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(displacement);
+        if (rank == mpi.rank) {
+            partition.first = displacement;
+            partition.count = rankCount;
+        }
+        displacement += rankCount;
+    }
+    return partition;
+}

codex
Implemented MPI distributed-memory parallelism in [roomsim.cpp](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/roomsim_gpt-5.6-terra-xhigh_mpi_r4/roomsim/roomsim.cpp:540) and required MPI linking in [CMakeLists.txt](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/roomsim_gpt-5.6-terra-xhigh_mpi_r4/roomsim/CMakeLists.txt:7).

- Dense matrix rows and radiosity traces are distributed across ranks.
- Each timestep uses an MPI all-gather of the bounded delay history.
- Results are deterministic across rank counts; only root gathers final distances for output.
- MPI is unconditional; executable remains `roomsim`.

Verified with CMake and MPI runs including 2 and 4 ranks. Default-size 4-rank validation passed with the same result hash as the original serial run.

 // Random Number Generation
 // ============================================================================
 
+// A counter-based generator makes samples independent of the rank that owns a
+// form-factor entry.  The counter is the position in the original row-major
+// sampling stream, so MPI process counts cannot change the random sequence.
 class RandomGenerator {
-    std::mt19937 rng;
-    std::uniform_real_distribution<val_t> dist;
+    uint64_t counter;
+
+    static uint64_t mix(uint64_t value) {
+        value += 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;
+        value = (value ^ (value >> 30)) * 0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9ULL;
+        value = (value ^ (value >> 27)) * 0x94d049bb133111ebULL;
+        return value ^ (value >> 31);
+    }
+
 public:
-    explicit RandomGenerator(uint32_t seed = 42) : rng(seed), dist(0.0f, 1.0f) {}
-    val_t rand() { return dist(rng); }
+    explicit RandomGenerator(uint64_t startCounter) : counter(startCounter) {}
+
+    val_t rand() {
+        // Use the top 24 bits, exactly representable in a float, for a
+        // uniformly distributed value in [0, 1).
+        constexpr val_t INV_2_POW_24 = 1.0f / 16777216.0f;
+        return static_cast<val_t>(mix(counter++) >> 40) * INV_2_POW_24;
+    }
 };

+struct MpiContext {
+    int rank = 0;
+    int size = 1;
+
+    bool isRoot() const { return rank == 0; }
 };
 
+struct RowPartition {
+    size_t first = 0;
+    size_t count = 0;
+    std::vector<int> counts;
+    std::vector<int> displacements;
+
+    int ownerOf(size_t globalRow) const {
+        for (size_t rank = 0; rank < counts.size(); ++rank) {
+            const size_t firstRow = static_cast<size_t>(displacements[rank]);
+            const size_t rowCount = static_cast<size_t>(counts[rank]);
+            if (globalRow >= firstRow && globalRow < firstRow + rowCount) {
+                return static_cast<int>(rank);
+            }
+        }
+        return 0;
+    }
+};
+
+RowPartition makeRowPartition(size_t numRows, const MpiContext& mpi) {
+    RowPartition partition;
+    partition.counts.resize(static_cast<size_t>(mpi.size));
+    partition.displacements.resize(static_cast<size_t>(mpi.size));
+
+    const size_t processCount = static_cast<size_t>(mpi.size);
+    const size_t baseCount = numRows / processCount;
+    const size_t remainder = numRows % processCount;
+    size_t displacement = 0;
+    for (int rank = 0; rank < mpi.size; ++rank) {
+        const size_t rankCount = baseCount + (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+        partition.counts[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(rankCount);
+        partition.displacements[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(displacement);
+        if (rank == mpi.rank) {
+            partition.first = displacement;
+            partition.count = rankCount;
+        }
+        displacement += rankCount;
+    }
+    return partition;
+}

-void computeFormFactors(SimulationState& state) {
-    printf("Computing form factors (Kij)...\n");
-    RandomGenerator rng(42);
+void computeFormFactors(SimulationState& state, const MpiContext& mpi) {
+    if (mpi.isRoot()) {
+        printf("Computing form factors (Kij) across %d MPI ranks...\n", mpi.size);
+    }
+
+    // Determine this contiguous row block's place in the conceptual serial
+    // random stream.  Exscan avoids an O(N^2) replicated setup pass.
+    uint64_t localDrawCount = 0;
+    for (size_t localI = 0; localI < state.numLocalTriangles; ++localI) {
+        const size_t i = state.firstLocalTriangle + localI;
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {
+            if (i != j && state.triangles[i].normal().dot(state.triangles[j].normal()) <= 0.99f) {
+                localDrawCount += 2 * NUM_RAYS;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+

Implemented MPI distributed-memory parallelism in [roomsim.cpp](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/roomsim_gpt-5.6-terra-xhigh_mpi_r4/roomsim/roomsim.cpp:540) and required MPI linking in [CMakeLists.txt](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/roomsim_gpt-5.6-terra-xhigh_mpi_r4/roomsim/CMakeLists.txt:7).

- Dense matrix rows and radiosity traces are distributed across ranks.
- Each timestep uses an MPI all-gather of the bounded delay history.
- Results are deterministic across rank counts; only root gathers final distances for output.
- MPI is unconditional; executable remains `roomsim`.

Verified with CMake and MPI runs including 2 and 4 ranks. Default-size 4-rank validation passed with the same result hash as the original serial run.
